🤖 RAG ANSWER (86.9s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   To apply for the Erasmus internship program, follow these steps:  1.
   **Eligibility**: Ensure you have a minimum GPA of 2.20 (undergraduate) or
   2.5 (graduate) and obtain an acceptance letter from a host organization
   for a minimum 2-month internship.  2. **Application Documents**: Submit
   your CV, transcript, and acceptance letter to the Internship Office by the
   October deadline.  3. **Selection**: Applicants are ranked based on their
   Erasmus+ score. A student list is approved by the Selection Committee in
   mid-April, with grant allocations determined sequentially.  4. **Pre-
   Departure Requirements**: Secure the signed Erasmus+ Internship Mobility
   Agreement from the host organization, complete the Learning Agreement,
   Grant Agreement, and Travel/Insurance documents, and submit them via
   SUFORM for academic approval.  5. **Online Application**: Finalize your
   application through the Online Application System, adhering to procedures
   outlined in the Erasmus+ Outgoing Student Procedure. Undergraduates must
   also provide language scores based on specified course grades.
------------------------------------------------------------

🤖 RAG ANSWER (78.3s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   Erasmus öğrenim hareketliliğinde hibe, hareketlilik süresi ve Ulusal Ajans
   tarafından belirlenen aylık hibe miktarına göre hesaplanır. Toplam hibe
   tutarı, hareketlilikte geçen hibeli ay sayısı ile ev sahibi ülke için
   belirlenen aylık hibe miktarının çarpımıyla belirlenir. Tamamlanmayan
   aylar, aylık birim masrafın 1/30’u ile gün sayısına göre hesaplanır.
   Öğrenci, hareketlilik süresinin tamamı için mali destek alıyorsa, ay ve
   gün sayısı toplam süreye eşit olur. Eğer sıfır-hibe dönemine sahipse,
   asgari hareketlilik süresi (öğrenim için 3 ay veya 1 akademik dönem)
   dikkate alınır. Özel ihtiyaçlara ilişkin harcamalar, destekleyici
   belgelerle birlikte geri ödenir. Hibenin ödemesi, hareketlilik başlamadan
   önce veya yurt dışına çıkmadan önce yapılır.
------------------------------------------------------------

================

🤖 RAG ANSWER (78.6s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   The Learning Agreement (LA) is prepared, signed, and submitted by the
   selected student within the timeframe announced by the university, with
   the approval of the exchange academic advisor. The advisor determines the
   equivalent credits, course types, and ECTS values for the selected courses
   at the partner institution. The student must ensure the courses taken
   abroad fulfill SU graduation requirements and inform the advisor of any
   changes before completing the term. The signed LA must be approved by the
   partner institution and submitted to the International Relations Office
   (IRO). For graduate students, the LA is prepared online in consultation
   with the advisor and submitted to IRO within the specified timeline. After
   signatures are obtained, the IRO forwards the LA to the Faculty
   Administrative Officer for approval. Students must also submit the signed
   LA, transcript, and Certificate of Attendance upon returning from the
   exchange program.
------------------------------------------------------------

🤖 RAG ANSWER (107.1s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   The disciplinary objection process at Sabancı University allows students
   to object to sanctions within **15 days** of notification by submitting a
   written petition to the University Administrative Board. The board
   examines the objection and either accepts or rejects the sanction within
   15 days. If rejected, the disciplinary board or supervisor reviews the
   objection considering the rejection grounds. For housing-related removal
   decisions, objections must be submitted within **5 working days** of
   notification, and the Disciplinary Board decides within **7 working
   days**. If a student appeals to the administrative jurisdiction and a stay
   of execution is granted, the Student Records (SR) notifies the
   disciplinary supervisor and relevant units in **1 day** upon receiving the
   court order. Notifications to units about disciplinary punishments are
   finalized within **3 days** after objection resolution if an objection is
   filed. Expelled students’ suspension periods are not counted toward their
   maximum study period, and suspended students must pay tuition for the
   semester they are suspended.
------------------------------------------------------------

   günden itibaren başlar. Cinsel taciz/saldırı gibi suçlar için ise eylemin
   işlendiği tarihten itibaren **en geç 2 yıl** içinde soruşturma

   need. It is funded through donations from domestic and international

🤖 RAG ANSWER (90.4s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   Lisansüstü programlarda tekrarlanan derslerden alınan en son not, GNO
   hesaplamasında dikkate alınır. Daha önce alınan dersin puanı GNO'dan
   çıkarılır ve tekrar edilen dersin en son notuna göre hesaplanan puan
   eklenir. "P", "S", "U", "T", "W" notları ortalama hesaplamasına
   katılmazken, "NA" notu "U" veya "F" olarak değerlendirilir. "I"
   (Incomplete) notu verilen derslerin SÜ kredisi, nihai harf notuna
   dönüştürülene kadar GNO hesaplamasına dahil edilmez. GNO, öğrencinin
   üniversitede kayıtlı olduğu tüm derslerin toplam puanının SÜ kredisi
   toplamına bölünmesiyle elde edilir. Tekrar edilen derslerin saydırılması
   otomatik olarak yapılır ve öğrenci için ek işlem gerekmez. Lisansüstü
   mezuniyet kriteri olarak GNO 3.00 ve üzeri olmalıdır.

================

🤖 RAG ANSWER (85.4s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   To open a graduate program at Sabancı University, the Graduate School
   Board, under the main coordination of the Vice President for Education
   (VP-E), submits an application to the Council of Higher Education (YOK)
   with the recommendation of the relevant Graduate School Board, the opinion
   of the University Education Council (UEC), approval from the Academic
   Council (AC), and final approval by the Board of Trustees. Education
   begins once YOK approves the program. For closure, the Graduate School
   Board proposes the closure, which requires the same approval process (UEC
   opinion, AC decision, Board of Trustees approval) and notification to YOK.
   The Student Records (SR) unit updates the YOKSIS system within one week of
   YOK’s approval for closures. Additionally, the SR sends the Board of
   Trustees’ decision to YOK within three business days after the Rector’s
   signature. The process for graduate program changes follows a separate
   procedure involving course adjustments. The opening process typically
   takes 3 months.
------------------------------------------------------------

[Gpa Calculation]
  • Requirement: alınan tüm dersler GNO hesaplarında kullanılır (lisans)
  • Requirement: all digits after comma are taken into consideration
  • Requirement: last grade for a repeated course is taken into account

🔗 RELATIONS (From Knowledge Graph):

  • Sabancı University Grad...
================

🤖 RAG ANSWER (67.8s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   Sabancı University calculates GPA (Grade Point Average) by dividing the
   total SU credit points earned in a term by the total SU credits of the
   courses taken in that term. For cumulative GPA (CGPA), the calculation
   includes all courses taken since enrollment. Undergraduate students use
   all courses taken, with the last grade of repeated courses counted, and
   results are displayed with two decimal places, though all decimal digits
   are considered in academic rankings. Graduate programs follow the same
   method, with CGPA calculated using all courses since enrollment, and
   repeated courses’ final grades included. Both undergraduate and graduate
   GPAs require SU credit-based calculations.
------------------------------------------------------------

🤖 RAG ANSWER (86.1s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   The Leave of Absence process requires students to apply within specific
   deadlines and pay a percentage of tuition fees based on the timing of the
   request. For undergraduate students, if the request is submitted by the
   end of the 4th week following the academic year's start, 25% of the
   semester fee is paid for a semester leave, and 25% for an academic year
   leave. Applications submitted after the 4th week but before the 7th week
   require 50% payment for a semester leave and 50% for an academic year
   leave. If the request is made after the 7th week up to the 10th week, full
   tuition fees are required. Students enrolled from 2020-2021 onward must
   pay the full fee for
------------------------------------------------------------

   özelliklere göre en yüksek puanlı öğrencilere eşleştirilir ve onay alınır.
   Onaylanmayan öğrenciler staja gidemez. Staj süresi en az 4 hafta olmak
   üzere, ev sahibi kuruluşun belirlediği maksimum süreye göre değişir. Staja

------------------------------------------------------------
   Undergraduate, graduate, and exchange students at Sabancı University can
   borrow up to 60 books for a period of 60 days. Book loans can be renewed
   unlimitedly for the same duration, provided no other user has requested
   the item. This limit applies specifically to the "General Book Collection"
   and "Large-Size Book Collection," as students cannot access the "Faculty
   Reserve Collection" or "Student Reserve Collection." Non-student groups,
   such as Package 2 or Package 3 users, have lower borrowing limits (e.g.,
   10 books for 30 days), but these do not pertain to regular student
   accounts.
------------------------------------------------------------

🤖 RAG ANSWER (66.4s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   The disciplinary penalties for students at Sabancı University include
   warnings, reprimands, temporary suspensions (1 week to 1 month or 1–2
   semesters), and dismissal. For warnings, reprimands, or short suspensions
   (up to one month), disciplinary proceedings must begin within **1 month**
   of the offense; for suspensions of 1–2 semesters or dismissal, proceedings
   must start within **3 months**. Students receiving suspension (1 week to 1
   month) or dismissal are **prohibited from entering the campus** during the
   penalty period and must **pay tuition for suspended semesters**. Repeated
   violations escalate penalties by one grade (e.g., a second offense results
   in a heavier punishment, but dismissal cannot be repeated). Dormitory-
   specific penalties include warnings, reprimands, or removal from dorms,
   with appeals to the Disciplinary Board allowed within **5 working days**
   of notification. Suspension periods do not count toward the maximum study
   duration, and scholarship continuation is evaluated under separate
   procedures.
------------------------------------------------------------

   Bir dersin kaç kez tekrar edilebilir?

📚 SOURCE   : Lisans Yönetmeliği Madde 30

✅ EXPECTED :
   Lisans öğrencileri daha önce geçer not aldıkları dersi, aldıkları dönemi
   izleyen en çok 3 dönem içinde tekrarlayabilir (izinli dönemler ve yaz
   dönemleri hariç). Başarısız olunan zorunlu dersler mezuniyete kadar tekrar
   edilerek başarılmalıdır. Lisansüstü öğrenciler için belirli bir tekrar

🤖 RAG ANSWER (85.0s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   Araç talep prosedürü şu şekilde işler:  1. Talep eden kişi, araç kullanım
   amacını belirterek **SUForm > Services > Transportation Form** altındaki
   **Vehicle Request / Temporary Replacement Car Request Form** doldurur.
   Talep, SÜ Yetkilisi tarafından onaylanır.  2. Araç en fazla **2 iş günü**
   süreyle tahsis edilir. Talep eden kişi, araç müsaitliği hakkında **1 iş
   gün önceden SÜ Yetkilisi ile iletişime geçer** ve rezervasyonu planlar.
   3. **Shuttle güzergahı** için araç talebi yapılmaz. Talep sebebi dışında
   araç kullanımı ya da üçüncü kişiye devretme yasaktır.  4. Aynı birimden
   birden fazla talep varsa, **ilk talep edene öncelik verilir**.  5. **Özel
   amaçlı araç talepleri** için **SUForm üzerinden Özel Amaçlı Araç Talep
   Formu** doldurulur. Talep eden birim, Ulaşım Hizmetleri'ne (UH) bilgi
   vermezse, sorumluluk ona aittir.  6. İptal durumunda:   - Hizmet günü **24
   saat önceden iptal** edilirse ücret talep edilmez.   - Hizmet saati **3
   saat önceden iptal** edilirse fiyatın yarısı, saatten sonra iptal edilirse
   tam bedel faturaya konur.  7. Aracın teslimi genellikle sabah yapılır.
   Akşam mesaisi veya onaylı şehir dışı seyahatler hariç, diğer rotalar için
   araç sabah teslim edil

   The graduation audit process at Sabancı University requires students to
   apply via SIS within the academic calendar's specified dates. Students
   must meet all graduation requirements, including completing required
   courses, credits, and GPA thresholds, which are monitored by both the
   student and their institute each semester. For thesis-based programs,
   stages like proposal, defense, and submission are tracked collaboratively.
   Institutes submit approved graduation lists to the Student Resources (SR)
   unit. If students fail to qualify in a semester, they must reapply the
   following semester. The SR and faculties must agree on audit outcomes, and
   diplomas are issued only after fulfilling all procedural steps, including
   registration or exchange program participation.

🤖 RAG ANSWER (61.0s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   The lost and found procedure on campus involves reporting found items by
   calling extension 3565 or delivering them to the Security Office in
   Building D2. Security officers prepare a two-copy Found Item Return Form,
   signed by both the finder and the officer, with the original retained by
   the Security Unit and the second copy given to the finder. Items are
   numbered (e.g., "01-24/01/01" format) and stored, with valuables separated
   into labeled bags. Unclaimed items are kept for one year. In cases of loss
   or theft on campus, individuals must notify the Security Unit via phone
   (5555) and the IT Unit through their ticketing system.
------------------------------------------------------------

🤖 RAG ANSWER (61.6s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   The disciplinary penalties for students include six types: **Warning**,
   **Reprimand**, **Suspension (1 week to 1 month)**, **Suspension for 1
   semester**, **Suspension for 2 semesters**, and **Dismissal from the
   Higher Education Institution**. These penalties are categorized based on
   severity, with warnings and reprimands being the least severe, followed by
   short-term suspensions, longer-term suspensions, and finally dismissal.
   The Disciplinary Supervisor decides on the first three penalties directly,
   while the latter two (suspension for semesters and dismissal) require
   review by the Disciplinary Board. Notifications to relevant units and
   institutions are mandatory for all penalties.

   Sabancı University provides several types of shuttle services:  1.
   **University Shuttle Services** for all members (students, staff) to and
   from the campus, operating on routes and schedules determined by
   Transportation Services (TS).  2. **Private Student Shuttles** to
   coordinate student transportation with external companies.  3. **On-Campus
   Ring Service** for internal campus transportation at designated times and
   routes.  4. **Special Purpose Shuttles** for academic, artistic, sports,
   or social activities requested by university units.  5. **Personnel
   Shuttles** for employees, interns, and subcontractors, adhering to TS
   regulations.  6. **Shift and Collection Services** for workers based on
   announced hours and routes.  7. **Temporary Vehicle Allocations** for
   short-term duties, managed under specific procedures.  Guests without
   SUCards must contact TS for shuttle access, and route/schedule changes
   require requests via SUTicket.

   Students staying in Sabancı University dormitories must adhere to the
   following rules:  - They are required to comply with all rules and
   regulations set by the Accommodation Services Unit, with violations
   potentially leading to disciplinary action.  - Students must not make
   noise or disturb others’ study/rest periods and cannot prevent another
   resident or staff from entering/exiting their room or offices.  -
   Unauthorized visitors or other students must not stay in their room after
   23:00, and all visitors must leave campus by the last shuttle time.  -
   Dormitories open 2 days before each academic term and must be vacated
   within 2 days after the term ends; failure to vacate twice without
   justification results in exclusion from dormitories in the next term.  -
   Students must use their assigned bunk, wardrobe, and cabinet, and any
   changes require prior approval from Dormitory Management to avoid
   responsibility for damages or losses.  - Departures must be notified via
   email to the Dormitories Unit with the exact date, locker, and caisson
   numbers; unreported vacancies after the deadline may result in belongings
   being discarded.

   itibaren 1 hafta içinde dosyalanır. Pandemi gibi özel durumlarda Sağlık

🤖 RAG ANSWER (50.1s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   Special event and catering services are arranged through the Food and
   Beverage Services (FBS) unit. The requesting unit must submit the "Special
   Event and Catering Request Form" on SUForm at least 30 days before the
   event. FBS prepares preliminary offers, including menus and
   specifications, based on the request. The unit approves the offer and
   initiates a procurement request. The Procurement and Operations Unit (POP)
   then selects an appropriate company and communicates the details to the
   unit and FBS. FBS coordinates with the selected company to ensure the
   event's operational needs are met. All events must comply with hygiene and
   service standards, and companies are evaluated every three years for
   suitability.
------------------------------------------------------------

------------------------------------------------------------
   Kampüs içi ulaşım hizmetleri, Ulaşım Hizmetleri (UH) tarafından belirlenen
   saat ve güzergâhlara göre düzenlenir. Günlük ring servisleri, Ana Kapı >
   Spor Merkezi/Gösteri Merkezi > DO > YBF Önü (Üniversite Merkezi) > Yurtlar
   > SUNUM > Shuttle Alanı > Ana Kapı > Spor Merkezi/Gösteri Merkezi
   rotasında sefer yapar. Hafta içi ve hafta sonu sefer saatleri farklılık
   gösterir. Üniversite shuttle servisleri, ilgili yasalar ve yönetmeliklere
   uygun olarak kampüs ile şehir arasında düzenli olarak sağlanır. Özel
   amaçlı servis talepleri (sanatsal, sportif, akademik etkinlikler gibi)
   üniversitenin birim ve fakülteleri tarafından ücret karşılığında yapılır.
   Özel öğrenci servislerinde öğrenci ile firma arasındaki koordinasyon UH
   tarafından sağlanır. Seyir halinde sürücüye müdahale, tartışmaya girmek
   veya dikkatini dağıtıcı davranışlar yasaktır ve bu durumlarda sözlü/yazılı
   uyarı uygulanır.

   The electric vehicle charging process at Sabancı University involves the
   use of Electric Vehicle Charging Stations located within the SU Tuzla
   campus. These stations are part of the electrical systems managed by the
   Electrical Works Department, which ensures their maintenance and
   operational functionality. Vehicles must be registered with the Security
   Directorate for entry, and registered vehicles can freely access the
   campus, including charging stations, through Gate A. However, if there is
   suspicion about the driver’s identity (e.g., student or parent) or if
   visitors are present in the vehicle, the driver must stop for identity
   verification. Vehicles are required to be used solely for the purpose
   stated in their request, and the process of returning a vehicle includes
   ensuring it is charged. Specific technical or procedural details about the
   charging operation itself (e.g., payment methods, charging duration) are
   not outlined in the provided context.

🤖 RAG ANSWER (87.1s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   The Health Center (HC) at Sabancı University provides psychiatric and
   psychological examinations for students and employees, accessible via
   appointment by calling 9923 or emailing healthcenter@sabanciuniv.edu.
   Students (excluding Exchange and Project Management undergraduates) are
   covered by health insurance from enrollment until graduation, allowing
   access to contracted institutions for private services with an HC
   Physician referral, though inpatient treatments (e.g., surgery) do not
   require referral. Preventive health services include mandatory chest
   X-rays and blood tests (e.g., Hepatitis A/B, MMR) during enrollment, with
   results recorded in the Health Information Form. Emergency services
   involve ambulance transportation to designated pandemic hospitals for
   urgent cases. Chronic disease medication previously prescribed by the HC
   can be reordered in person or via email. Foreign students have a 40%
   participation rate in insurance, and coverage does not extend abroad.

------------------------------------------------------------
   To be eligible for the SU President’s Office Research Fund, full-time
   faculty members must have started their activities at the university
   within the last two years and completed at least six months of service.
   They must propose projects compatible with their ongoing or initial
   research activities and ensure the budget prioritizes infrastructure
   investments (e.g., devices, consumables, outsourcing) over personnel
   salaries. Applicants must commit to submitting at least two new documented
   external grant applications or securing one guaranteed external financing
   by the project’s end and publishing high-impact scientific articles. The
   project duration cannot exceed 36 months, with a maximum theoretical
   research budget of 300,000 TL and experimental research budget of 600,000
   TL. Faculty members are limited to applying only once for this fund.
